var doofinder_script ='//cdn.doofinder.com/media/js/doofinder-classic.7.latest.min.js';
(function(d,t){var f=d.createElement(t),s=d.getElementsByTagName(t)[0];f.async=1;
f.src=('https:'==location.protocol?'https:':'http:')+doofinder_script;
f.setAttribute('charset','utf-8');
s.parentNode.insertBefore(f,s)}(document,'script'));

var search_hash = {
  ca:"af7023315c40cbf8dfd0895c99469d94",
  de:"25040c90c1fb2ffb6a266e445d67f658", 
  en:"7b2ee43ec6c84605dde26a5a29cf5dc9",
  en_gbp: "8f70b81a8c906adfab3cfd44248b9804",
  es: "47a1787fd24200a0ec6e8dc14db412d4",
  fr: "7c4bb86327faa2564252ef29e8a2428a",
  it: "8b376ea007fac99dc4c2d78fc812bba0",
  pt: "5574203b2564ac41ffda654a0757958b"
  };

var lang = {
  ca:"ca",
  de:"de",
  en:"en",
  es:"es",
  fr:"fr",
  it:"it",
  pt:"pt"
}

var currency = {
  GBP: "GBP",
  EUR: "EUR"
}

var country = {
  GB: "GB"
}

var hash = search_hash.en
var dfClassicLayers = [{
  "hashid": hash,
  "zone": "eu1",
  "display": {
    "lang": lang.en,
    "width": "100%",
    "align": "center",
    "template": document.getElementById('df-layer-template').innerHTML,
    "results": {
      "template": document.getElementById('df-results-template').innerHTML,
      "initialLayout": 'grid'
    },
    "captureLength": 0
  },
  "queryInput": ".input-home-df",
  "mobile": {
    "toggleInput": "#search_icon",
    
    "display": {
      "initialSearch": false,
      "results": {
      "template": document.getElementById('df-results-template-mobile').innerHTML,
    }
    }
  },
  "callbacks": {
    loaded: function(instance) {
      var controller = instance.layer.controller;
      controller.processors.push(function(response){
        response.results = response.results.map(function(result){
            let iva =  parseInt('20.0')
            iva = iva == 0 ? 0 : iva;
            result["g:pvp_final"] = parseFloat(result["g:pvp"]).toFixed(2)
            result["g:pvd1_final"] = parseFloat(result["g:pvd1"]).toFixed(2)
            result["g:pvd2_final"] = parseFloat(result["g:pvd2"]).toFixed(2)
            result["g:pvd3_final"] = parseFloat(result["g:pvd3"]).toFixed(2)
            
            if(result["g:offer"] != "0"){
              let offer = parseFloat(result["g:offer"]) / 100;
              result["g:pvp_final"] = ((parseFloat(result["g:pvp"])- parseFloat(result["g:pvp"]) * offer) ).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd1_final"] = ((parseFloat(result["g:pvd1"])- parseFloat(result["g:pvd1"]) * offer)).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd2_final"] = ((parseFloat(result["g:pvd2"])- parseFloat(result["g:pvd2"]) * offer)).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd3_final"] = ((parseFloat(result["g:pvd3"])- parseFloat(result["g:pvd3"]) * offer)).toFixed(2)
            }
            let taxrate = parseFloat(iva) / 100;
            result["g:pvp_iva"] = ((parseFloat(result["g:pvp_final"]) + parseFloat(result["g:pvp_final"]) * taxrate)).toFixed(2)
            result["g:product_ref"] = result["id"].substr(0,5);
            if(currency.GBP == "GBP"){
              let exchange = 0.886500000000
              result["g:pvp_final"] = (result["g:pvp_final"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd1_final"] = (result["g:pvd1_final"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd2_final"] = (result["g:pvd2_final"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd3_final"] = (result["g:pvd3_final"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvp"] = (result["g:pvp"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd1"] = (result["g:pvd1"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd2"] = (result["g:pvd2"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvd3"] = (result["g:pvd3"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
              result["g:pvp_iva"] = (result["g:pvp_iva"]* exchange).toFixed(2)
            }
          return result;
        });
        $('body').css("overflow","hidden");
        return response;
      });
      document.addEventListener("keydown", event => {
        if(event.key === "Escape") {
            $('body').css("overflow", "unset");
        }
      });
      $(".df-icon--close").click(function (e) {
        $('body').css("overflow", "unset");
      });
    }
  }
}];
</script>

<div class="modal-body">
<div style="margin-bottom:15px;"><strong class="grey">Why different prices? Which one is mine?</strong></div>
<div class="blue bold">PSP: Price of Sale for the Public</div>
<div class="card-body">Price applied to customers (individuals or companies) that are NOT IT distributors or perform any activity related to technology.</div>
<div class="blue bold">PSD1, PSD2 and PSD3: Price of Sale for the Distributor</div>
<div class="card-body">Price that will be applied to clients that demonstrate an activity related to the IT sector, either at a distribution or service level.</div>
